{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["An empirical equation used to calculate the total power loss (core losses) per unit volume in magnetic materials when subjected to external sinusoidally varying magnetic flux: P_v=k·fᵃ·Bᵇ where P_v is the time average power loss per unit volume in milliwatts per cubic centimeter, f is frequency in kilohertz, and B is the peak magnetic flux density; k, a, and b, called the Steinmetz coefficients, are material parameters generally found empirically from the material's B-H hysteresis curve by curve fitting."],"synonyms":[{"word":"power equation"}]}],"upstream_line":9371205,"upstream_offset":22937625104,"upstream_record_sha256":"8209f8aaa6e7bae347e37049905e52d9e4880395ec1b3cb02e06c30698007a3c","word":"Steinmetz's equation"}

{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"jars","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A clashing or discordant set of sounds, particularly with a quivering or vibrating quality."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A quivering or vibrating movement or sensation resulting from something being shaken or struck."],"synonyms":[{"word":"jolt"}],"tags":["also","countable","figuratively"]},{"glosses":["A sense of alarm or dismay."],"tags":["broadly","countable"]},{"glosses":["The effect of something contradictory or discordant; a clash."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A disagreement, a dispute, a quarrel; (uncountable) contention, discord; quarrelling."],"tags":["archaic","countable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"jär"},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɑː/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɑː(ɹ)]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɑɹ/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɑɹ]","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɐː/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɐː(ɹ)]","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":214247,"upstream_offset":1037358388,"upstream_record_sha256":"7bcd31e06f225765529fcc32b4aa8a31eea9c68a0c9e44746a3bff38d1c17bc3","word":"jar"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"jars","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"jarring","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"jarred","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"jarred","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He hit it with a hammer, hoping he could jar it loose.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To knock, shake, or strike sharply, especially causing a quivering or vibrating movement."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To harm or injure by such action."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I think the accident jarred him, as he hasn’t got back in a car since.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To shock or surprise."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To act in disagreement or opposition, to clash, to be at odds with; to interfere; to dispute, to quarrel."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The clashing notes jarred on my ears.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To (cause something to) give forth a rudely tremulous or quivering sound; to (cause something to) sound discordantly or harshly."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["To quiver or vibrate due to being shaken or struck."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of the appearance, form, style, etc., of people and things: to look strangely different; to stand out awkwardly from its surroundings; to be incongruent."],"tags":["figuratively","intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"jär"},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɑː/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɑː(ɹ)]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɑɹ/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɑɹ]","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/d͡ʒɐː/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[d͡ʒɐː(ɹ)]","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":214248,"upstream_offset":1037369938,"upstream_record_sha256":"fdd3c3371eb33488dbbb495c0e5715f165f9dbf8c3534eb2221e42ff1d53d3a6","word":"jar"}
